PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES                                                                         主要業務

The principal activity of the Company is investment holding. The Company’s                   本 公 司 的 主 要 業 務 為 投 資 控 股。本 公 司 的 附 屬 公
subsidiaries are principally engaged in the manufacture and sale of cornstarch, lysine,      司 主 要 從 事 製 造 及 銷 售 玉 米 澱 粉、賴 氨 酸、澱 粉
starch-based sweetener, modified starch and ancillary corn-based and corn-refined            糖、變性澱粉、玉米製副產品及玉米深加工產品。
products. Details of the principal activities of the Company’s subsidiaries are set out in   有關本公司附屬公司的主要業務詳情載於經審核
note 15 to the audited consolidated financial statements.                                    合併財務報表附註15。

in the domestic market and the indirect competition with other replaceable products.     問 題 以 及 與 其 他 可 替 代 產 品 的 間 接 競 爭。二 零

around the globe. This trend drove down the market prices of finished goods which        不景氣。這一趨勢導致大豆、小麥及其他穀物製成
were derived from soybean, wheat and other grains. The end-users might shift             品的市場價格下跌。出於對價格波動的考慮，終端

FINANCIAL MANAGEMENT AND TREASURY POLICY                                                財務管理及庫務政策

The Group adopts a conservative approach for cash management and investment on          本集團對並無指定用途的資金採取保守的現金管
uncommitted funds. The primary purpose of our financial management and treasury         理 及 投 資 策 略。我 們 財 務 管 理 及 庫 務 政 策 的 主 要

Our risk measurement philosophy                                                           我們的風險管理理念

An effective risk review system could help an entity minimise and manage risks            有效的風險檢討系統可幫助實體將風險降至最低
efficiently. Our risk management and internal control systems are not one-off             及 有 效 地 管 理 風 險。風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 系 統 並
arrangements; instead they are integrated into our daily business operations              非一次過的安排，相反，該等系統是我們的日常業
and decision-making process. The objective of upholding a high standard of risk           務 營 運 及 決 策 過 程 的 一 部 份。維 持 高 水 準 風 險 管
management and internal control is to guarantee the sustainability of the Group’s         理及內部監控的目的是保證本集團可持續發展。
development.


The Board has the overall responsibility for the Group’s risk management and internal     董事會對本集團風險管理及內部監控系統承擔整
control systems. These systems are monitored by the Board through the assistance          體責任。該等系統由董事會在審核委員會、企劃部
of the Audit Committee, Corporate Strategy and Development Department and                 及內部審核部協助下進行監察。
Internal Audit Department.


Identifying risks                                                                         確認風險

The management uses risk scorecard to rank and compare each identified risk and its       管理層採用風險計分卡對本集團所面對的各項已
related impact posing to the Group. Through this analysis process, all identified risks   識 別 風 險 及 其 相 關 影 響 進 行 評 級 及 比 較。透 過 是
are presented to the Board in an effective and comprehensible manner. The Board           項 分 析 過 程，所 有 已 識 別 的 風 險 會 以 有 效 及 易 於
reviews the risks and mitigation tools every year.                                        理 解 之 方 式 向 董 事 會 呈 報。董 事 會 每 年 檢 討 風 險
                                                                                          及舒緩風險的工具。


How we manage risk                                                                        管理風險的方法

Risk and control responsibility must be well-defined and delegated to each staff. Each    我們須明確界定風險及監控責任並落實到各個員
staff must understand their duty and recognise their responsibility. A well-defined       工。各員工均須了解其職責，並深明其責任。界定
control prevention system is a key to success. Our risk prevention mechanism is as        明 確 的 監 控 防 範 系 統 乃 成 功 的 關 鍵。我 們 的 風 險
follows:                                                                                  防範機制如下：


Risk ownership          The executive management team is assigned the responsibility for managing risks and monitoring the
                        implementation of risk mitigation policies. In addition, the team heads are required to pledge themselves to ensure
                        appropriate control procedures are in place within their respective teams to the Chief Executive Officer once a
                        year.
風險承擔                    執行管理團隊負責管理風險並監管風險舒緩政策的落實情況。此外，各小組主管須每年向行政總裁承諾確保其
                        各自的團隊已制訂合適的監控程序。

Monitoring              A monthly management report in respect to the operation, market and safety issues is circulated amongst the
                        management team. It allows the management to stay alert on potential risks in different seasons.
監管                      每月就經營、市場及安全事宜向管理團隊提交管理報告，令管理層對不同季節的潛在風險保持警惕。


Compliance review       Internal Audit Department considers the review areas for the coming year and discusses its plan with the Audit
                        Committee. It also carries out ad-hoc tasks on specific areas which required by the Board or the management.
                        The internal audit report will be discussed with the respective department head and reviewed by the Board
                        respectively.
合規審查                    內部審核部門考慮來年的審查範圍，並與審核委員會討論其計劃。其亦按董事會或管理層的要求對特定領域進
                        行臨時審核。內部審核報告將與各部門主管討論，並由董事會審閱。

風險審閱報告

Challenges                                                                               挑戰

After the end of the COVID-19 pandemic towards the end of 2022, people looked            於二零二二年底COVID-19疫情結束後，人們期待
forward to having an energetic and prosperous 2023. However, this hope burst             著一個充滿活力及繁榮的二零二三年。然而，全球
due to global inflation, dampening market demand and geopolitical tensions.              通貨膨脹、市場需求抑制、地緣政治緊張等因素使
These unstable factors hindered the global economic recovery. The Group, as a            這一希望破滅。該等不穩定因素阻礙了經濟復甦。

Risk Management and Internal Control                                                          風險管理及內部監控

Each company and industry is associated with specific inevitable risks. We cannot             各 公 司 及 行 業 均 無 可 避 免 面 對 特 定 的 風 險。我 們
avoid them, but we adopt the best and responsible approach to manage and                      無 法 避 免 風 險，但 我 們 採 取 最 佳 及 負 責 的 方 法 處
overcome them. Our risk management and internal control systems have been in                  理 及 克 服 風 險。我 們 的 風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 系 統
place for many years, which provide the assurance of operation efficiency, work               已運作多年，為營運效率、工作安全及財產保障提

committee for reviewing risk management and internal control systems. However, the            部監控系統的專責委員會。然而，董事會負責監察

the Group. The risk management and internal systems are reviewed on an ongoing                及 內 部 監 控 系 統，以 確 保 其 於 識 別 業 務 風 險 時 提

assurance in relation to the identification of business risks. The Board is satisfied that,   察，董 事 會 信 納 本 集 團 現 有 的 風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監
based on the best information available and its own observations, the present risk            控系統乃屬有效適當。
management and internal control systems of the Group are effective and adequate.

for assessment and consideration of implementing a corresponding mitigation plan              風險進行評估，以及考慮實施相應的舒緩計劃（如
where appropriate. However, it is possible that certain risks remain undetected or            適用）。然而，仍有可能出現若干未能發現或識別
unidentified and risks currently identified as immaterial may eventually turn out to          的 風 險，或 現 時 識 別 為 對 本 集 團 不 重 大 但 最 終 成

with our business and industry, our risk management and internal control systems              干 風 險 屬 無 法 避 免，我 們 的 風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控

Basis for Opinion                                                                           意見基礎

We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing                   我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審計準則
(“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under those standards are              （「香港審計準則」）進行審計。我們在該等準則項
further described in the Auditor’s Responsibilities for the Audit of the Consolidated       下 的 責 任 在 本 報 告「核 數 師 對 合 併 財 務 報 表 的 審
Financial Statements section of our report. We are independent of the Group in              計責任」一節進一步闡述。根據香港會計師公會的
accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (the               職業會計師道德守則（「守則」），我們獨立於                貴
“Code”) and we have fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with         集 團，並 已 按 照 守 則 履 行 其 他 道 德 責 任。我 們 相
the Code. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and             信，我們已獲得足夠及適當的審計憑證，為我們的
appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.                                             審計意見提供基礎。


Key Audit Matters                                                                           關鍵審計事項

Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional judgement, were of most       關 鍵 審 計 事 項 是 根 據 我 們 專 業 判 斷 所 認 為，對 我
significance in our audit of the consolidated financial statements of the current period.   們 審 計 本 期 間 合 併 財 務 報 表 最 為 重 要 的 事 項。該
These matters were addressed in the context of our audit of the consolidated financial      等事項已於我們審計整份合併財務報表及就其出

     2.2    Subsidiaries                                                                    2.2   附屬公司
            A subsidiary is an entity over which the Group has control. The Group                 附屬公司為本集團對其存有控制
            controls an entity when the Group is exposed to, or has rights to,                    權 的 實 體。倘 本 集 團 須 承 擔 或 享
            variable returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability             有參與實體之可變回報風險及權
            to affect those returns through its power over the entity. Subsidiaries               利，以 及 可 運 用 其 對 該 實 體 之 權
            are consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the                 力 以 影 響 有 關 回 報 時，則 對 實 體
            Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                     存 有 控 制 權。附 屬 公 司 於 控 制 權
                                                                                                  轉 移 至 本 集 團 日 期 起 合 併 入 賬，
                                                                                                  並於控制權終止日期起不再合併
                                                                                                  入賬。


            Intra-group transactions, balances and unrealised gains on                            集 團 內 公 司 間 交 易、結 餘 及 集 團
            transactions between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised                       內公司間交易的未變現收益均予
            losses are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence                   對 銷。未 變 現 虧 損 亦 會 對 銷，惟
            of an impairment of the transferred asset. When necessary, amounts                    有證據顯示交易所轉移資產出現
            reported by subsidiaries have been adjusted to conform with the                       減 值 除 外。如 有 需 要，附 屬 公 司
            Group’s accounting policies.                                                          呈 報 的 金 額 已 經 進 行 調 整，以 與
                                                                                                  本集團的會計政策保持一致。


            Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income of                    附屬公司的損益及其他綜合收益

            non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of subsidiaries                 非 控 股 股 東 權 益 應 佔。即 使 會 令

            a significant increase in credit risk. Note 3.1(b) set out the details how             的 虧 損 撥 備 並 不 重 大。所 應 用 的
            the Group determines whether there has been a significant increase                     減值方法視乎信貸風險是否已經

            not subject to ECL assessment.                                                         期 貨 合 約）毋 須 接 受 預 期 信 貸 虧

            to provide loss allowance at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs for                      簡 單 方 法，於 首 次 確 認 金 融 資 產
            its financial assets at initial recognition and through its life of the                及在資產整段生命年期內按相當
            asset. A provision matrix is determined based on historical overdue                    於生命年期預期信貸虧損的金額
            pattern, shared risk characteristics, probability-weighted estimate of                 作 出 虧 損 撥 備。撥 備 矩 陣 乃 根 據
            credit losses and is adjusted for forward-looking estimates. At each                   過 往 逾 期 模 式、共 有 風 險 特 質 及
            reporting date the above parameters are updated.                                       機 率 加 權 估 計 信 貸 風 險 釐 定，並
                                                                                                   就 前 瞻 估 計 作 出 調 整。上 述 參 數
                                                                                                   於各報告日期更新。

            In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly since                 評估信貸風險自初步確認以來是
            initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a default occurring                否 顯 著 上 升 時，本 集 團 會 比 較 金
            on the financial instrument as at the reporting date with the risk                     融工具於報告日期發生違約的風
            of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of                   險與於初步確認日期發生的違約
            initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group considers                    風 險。作 出 評 估 時，本 集 團 會 考
            both quantitative and qualitative information that is reasonable and                   慮合理及具支持性的定量及定性
            supportable, including historical experience and forward-looking                       資 料，包 括 毋 須 付 出 不 必 要 之 成
            information that is available without undue cost or effort.                            本或努力即可獲得的過往經驗及
                                                                                                   前瞻性資料。

            The following information is taken into account when assessing                         評估信貸風險是否顯著上升時會
            whether credit risk has increased significantly:                                       考慮以下資料：

            and credit risk ratings.                                                                  風險評級。

            which the recovery occurs.

     2.20   Provisions                                                                    2.20   撥備
            Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or                      當本集團因過往事件而須負上
            constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that              現 有 的 法 定 或 推 定 責 任，並 且 可
            an outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation;                   能 須 流 失 資 源 以 履 行 責 任，而 金
            and the amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not                       額 亦 能 夠 可 靠 估 計 時，會 確 認 撥
            recognised for future operating losses.                                              備。未來經營虧損不會確認撥備。

            Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an              倘 有 多 項 類 似 責 任 出 現，則 會 整
            outflow will be required in settlement is determined by considering the              體考慮責任所屬類別以釐定履行
            class of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the               責 任 會 否 導 致 資 源 流 失。即 使 同
            likelihood of an outflow with respect to any one item included in the                一類別責任內任何一項造成資源
            same class of obligations may be small.                                              流 失 的 可 能 性 不 高，仍 會 確 認 撥
                                                                                                 備。

            Provisions are measured at the present value of the expenditures                     撥備按為履行責任預計所需開支
            expected to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate                的 現 值 計 量，計 算 此 等 現 值 使 用
            that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money                  的稅前貼現率能夠反映對貨幣時
            and the risks specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision              間價值及該責任特有的風險的市
            due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.                            場 評 估。時 間 流 逝 導 致 撥 備 金 額
                                                                                                 的增加會確認為利息開支。

     3.1   Financial risk factors                                                                         3.1   財務風險因素
           The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks: market                             本集團的業務使其面對多種財務

                                                                                                               。於公認
                   Level 1 inputs.                                                                      商品市場的期貨報價屬
                                                                                                        第一級輸入數據。


           –       Level 2 inputs are those, other than quoted prices included in              –        第二級輸入數據為不包

                                                                                                        可直接或間接觀察的輸

12   Income tax expense (Continued)                                                     12   所得稅支出（續）

     The Group’s major business is in the PRC. Under the law of the PRC on                   本集團之主要業務於中國進行。根據中國
     EIT and its Implementation Regulation, the tax rate of the PRC subsidiaries             企 業 所 得 稅 法 及 其 實 施 細 則，中 國 附 屬
     is 25% for both years, except for one subsidiary of the Group which is                  公司於兩個年度之稅率均為25%。惟本集
     recognised as high technology enterprise is entitled to enjoy a preferential EIT        團一間獲認可為高技術企業的附屬公司
     rate of 15% (2022:15%).                                                                 除 外，該 公 司 可 享 有15%（二 零 二 二 年：
                                                                                             15% ）的優惠企業所得稅率。


     No provision for Hong Kong Profits Tax has been made as the Group entities’             由於本集團的實體於兩個年度內並無在
     profit neither arose in nor was derived from Hong Kong during both years.               香港產生或賺取利潤，故並無就香港利得
                                                                                             稅作出撥備。


     Pursuant to the law of the PRC on EIT and its Implementation Regulation,                根據中國企業所得稅法及其實施細則，對
     non-PRC resident enterprises are levied withholding tax at 10% (unless                  非中國居民企業應收中國企業自二零零
     reduced by tax treaties/arrangements) on dividends receivable from PRC                  八年一月一日起所賺取利潤作出的股息，
     enterprises for profits earned since 1 January 2008. The Group adopted the              按10％的 稅 率 徵 收 預 扣 稅（除 非 稅 收 協
     10% withholding tax rate for PRC withholding tax purposes during the years              定╱安排訂明較低的稅率）。於截至二零
     ended 31 December 2023 and 2022.                                                        二三年及二零二二年十二月三十一日止
                                                                                             年度，本集團已就中國預扣稅採納10％的
                                                                                             預扣稅稅率。
